# Progress & Handoff

_Last updated: 2026-11-13 — **Milestone 17 (Game Boy and Game Boy Color) in progress:** spec approved by the user, **17a (the engine on the PC) done** — plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-12-milestone-17a-gb-core.md`, 12 tasks; **17b (the app + release 1.1.0): plan written 2026-11-13 (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-13-milestone-17b-gb-app.md`, 10 tasks), Task 1 next**; branch `milestone-17`. Before it: milestone 16 done — DreamBoy 1.0.0 is public at https://github.com/tengencode/DreamBoy (release v1.0.0). Milestones 1–16 done (10c cancelled); commit hashes from before 2026-10-24 refer to the private archive `gba_emulator`._

## Goal
- **Project:** a Game Boy Advance emulator for Android, similar to Pizza Boy. Core, graphics (OpenGL ES), sound (AAudio — changed from OpenSL ES by user decision 2026-09-26), input and game loop in C + ARM64 assembly; a thin Kotlin app for menus/settings.
- **Milestone 1 (done):** emulator core on the PC — CPU, memory, built-in BIOS, event timer, command-line test runner; passes the jsmolka `arm`, `thumb`, `memory`, `bios` ROMs.
- **Milestone 2 (done):** graphics (PPU) + DMA + timers + PC viewer. Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-24-milestone-2-ppu-dma-timers-design.md`. Done when the 3 jsmolka ppu ROMs + 19 tonc demos match approved screenshots and `gbaview` runs demos live. Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-24-milestone-2-ppu-dma-timers.md`.
- **Milestone 3 (done, tag `milestone-3c2`), split into parts (user decision 2026-09-25):** **3a sound** (done, tag `milestone-3a`), **3b save chips** (done, tag `milestone-3b`), **3b-2 cartridge extras** (done, tag `milestone-3b2`; plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-25-milestone-3b2-cart-extras.md`, 6 tasks; design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-25-milestone-3b2-cart-extras-design.md` — done when unit tests replaying each game's access routine pass and the user's own game A ROM reaches the main menu without "The internal battery has run dry"), **3c** split again (user decision 2026-09-25) into **3c-1 right values** (done, tag `milestone-3c1`; design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-25-milestone-3c1-values-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-25-milestone-3c1-values.md` (10 tasks) — done when mGBA suite groups Memory, I/O read, Shifter, Carry, Multiply long, BIOS math, SIO register R/W are all at 100% (SIO timing moved to 3c-2 by the user), plus keypad IRQ, link port 'no cable' and Stop mode, and the user sees the scores in `gbaview`) and **3c-2 right timing** (design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-25-milestone-3c2-timing-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-25-milestone-3c2-timing.md` (10 tasks); per-instruction timing like mGBA — **finish line revised by the user to mGBA level**: Timing ≥ 1708/2020, DMA 1244/1244, Timer IRQ ≥ 70, Timer count-up / Misc / SIO timing as high as a time-boxed tuning task gets them (aim count-up ≥ 610)) — **done, tag `milestone-3c2`**. 3b design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-25-milestone-3b-saves-design.md` — done when jsmolka sram/flash64/flash128/none + our own EEPROM ROMs pass and the user sees green then blue in `gbaview` (save written and reloaded). Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-25-milestone-3b-saves.md` (8 tasks). 3a design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-25-milestone-3a-sound-design.md` (approved) — done when our own sound test ROMs + Tonc `snd1_demo` match approved audio fingerprints and pitches, and the user confirms the sound in `gbaview`. Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-25-milestone-3a-sound.md` (10 tasks).
- **Milestone 4 (done): Android platform layer**, split into 3 parts (user decision 2026-09-26): **4a app + picture** (done, tag `milestone-4a`; design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-milestone-4a-app-picture-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-26-milestone-4a-app-picture.md`) → **4b sound** (done, tag `milestone-4b`: design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-milestone-4b-sound-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-26-milestone-4b-sound.md` (6 tasks) — done when games play with sound on the Pixel 9 emulator (AAudio, cubic rate conversion to the phone's rate, the sound pacing the game; clock pacing only without sound), all PC and on-device tests pass, and the user's listening check passes) → **4c controls + save files + lifecycle** (done: design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-milestone-4c-controls-saves-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-26-milestone-4c-controls-saves.md` (6 tasks) — done when the user plays their game A hack with only the touch buttons, saves, quits, reopens with Continue and the save is there, imports their PC `.sav`, and Home/back loses nothing; all tests pass) → **4d rumble + sensors** (done: design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-milestone-4d-rumble-sensors-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-27-milestone-4d-rumble-sensors.md` (6 tasks) — done when game D reacts to turning the user's real phone and its rumble is felt, Q/E work on the emulator, tilt and light are proven by tests and Claude's check with the emulator's virtual sensors, and all tests pass).
- **Milestone 5 (done): save states, rewind, fast-forward, cheats**, split (user decision 2026-09-27) into **5a save states + in-game menu** (done, tag `milestone-5a`: design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-27-milestone-5a-save-states-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-27-milestone-5a-save-states.md` (6 tasks) — done when the user saves/loads states in game A, Continue resumes the exact moment after Home or a force-close, Restart game works, and all 32 fingerprint ROMs replay to their approved hashes from a state loaded into a new core, PC and emulator) → **5b fast-forward + rewind** (done, tag `milestone-5b`: design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-27-milestone-5b-fast-forward-rewind-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-27-milestone-5b-fast-forward-rewind.md` (7 tasks) — done when the user, in game A on the emulator, fast-forwards by hold/tap at the menu's 2×/4×/8×/Max with sped-up sound (Max silent), rewinds by holding ⟲ and plays on, R2/L2 and the keyboard keys (F a tap switch, R held) work, and all 32 fingerprints replay exactly through a rewind, PC (CTest 120) and emulator) → **5c cheats** (done, tag `milestone-5c`: design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-28-milestone-5c-cheats-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-milestone-5c-cheats.md` (7 tasks) — done when the user, in game A on the emulator, adds real Action Replay and CodeBreaker codes from a cheat site and they work, switching one off stops it, the switched-on ones are on again after reopening, and all tests pass: the cipher and step tests, published game A codes' effects in Claude's PC check, CTest 120 unchanged).
- **Milestone 7 (done): ARM64 assembly speedups**, split (user decision 2026-10-02) into **7a measuring + C speed-ups** (done, tag `milestone-7a`: design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-02-milestone-7a-speed-design.md` — goal: run well on cheap and old phones; done when every speed scene meets the finish-line speed agreed with the user after the prototype's measurements (aim ≥ 4× real time on the user's phone's small core), every fingerprint is unchanged on the PC, the emulator and the user's phone, and the user's check on their phone passes — met: game A 2.04×, game B 1.22×, game C 3.24×, game D 1.63×, m7 4.48×, snd1 12.28× on phone B's small core) → **7b ARM64 assembly + NEON** (done, tag `milestone-7b`: design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-03-milestone-7b-asm-design.md` — the CPU loop in hand-written assembly, remembered instructions, NEON drawing and sound through C intrinsics, each kept only if it measures as a win on phone B's small core; done when the finish line agreed with the user after the prototype's measurements is met (aim ≥ 4×), every fingerprint is unchanged on the PC, the emulator and phone B with the fast pieces on and with `GBA_FORCE_GENERIC`, and the user's check on phone B passes — met: game A 4.65×, game B 3.52×, game C 6.41×, game D 3.37×, m7 5.90×, snd1 24.33× on phone B's small core).
- **Milestone 8 (done): app polish + skins** (user request 2026-10-03, after all 7 milestones of the design were done), split (user decision) into **8a the new look** (done, tag `milestone-8a`: design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-03-milestone-8a-app-look-design.md` — the 'Cartridge grey' colours instead of Android's default purple, the game list as a grid with a Continue card, a clearer pause menu and Settings (switches and choice buttons instead of pop-ups), real icons, no "…" or "&" in any label; done when the user finds the screens easier and nicer, no "…" anywhere, everything works as before and all tests pass — met) → **8b console skins** (done, tag `milestone-8b`: spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-04-milestone-8b-console-skins-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-04-milestone-8b-console-skins.md` (6 tasks) — 13 skins colouring the body round the picture and the touch buttons; done when Claude's emulator check passes and all tests pass, no user check — met) (touch buttons in the colours of the special-edition handhelds; its own spec after 8a).

Each with its own spec and plan.
- **Milestone 11 (done, tag `milestone-11`; added by the user 2026-10-04): backup and restore** — branch `milestone-11`, spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-04-milestone-11-backup-design.md` — brainstormed 2026-10-04: a full backup (not per game) as one plain `.zip` (`GBA Emulator backup <date time>.zip`) with saves, save states, settings and controls (settings, keys, game settings, layouts), cheats, imported skins and the user's own box-art choices; not the BIOS file (licensing), the game list / folder link, ROMs or downloaded box art; made by hand with Android's save-as picker, plus optional automatic backups into a picked folder (after quitting a game, at most once a day, newest 5 kept); restore replaces everything after a safety copy, with Undo restore; Android's own backup turned off (`allowBackup=false`); no password; done when all tests pass and Claude's emulator check passes — **no user check** (user decision).
- **Milestone 12 (done, tag `milestone-12`; added by the user 2026-10-05): the UI overhaul — "Game Pak"** — branch `milestone-12`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-05-milestone-12-game-pak-ui.md` (8 tasks), prototype on `proto-12` (tried by the user: "It's beautiful, great work"), spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-05-milestone-12-game-pak-ui-design.md`, shaped with the **impeccable** skill (the user asked for it): product record `PRODUCT.md`, direction record `.impeccable/surfaces/app-src-main-java-com-tengen-gbaemu-screens-kt.md` (seed `3bf0b5f2`). The user's answers: audience "me now, public later"; today's look is generic with no personality; **replace the whole look**; scope = game list + first start, in-game screens, Settings, app icon + launch screen; direction **Game Pak Labels** (games as cartridges with their picture as the label, the last-played one seated in the slot; tapping Continue "inserts" it); light/dark **follows the phone** (grey plastic / smoked plastic); the accent **follows the console skin** (default indigo); the **phone's own fonts** (Roboto Black Italic, Roboto Condensed); **prototype first, the user tries it**, then the plan. Done when the user's check, Claude's emulator check (light/dark, upright/sideways, font 1.3×), the impeccable finish review (`ship`) and all tests pass, and `DESIGN.md` is written.
- **Milestone 13 (done, tag `milestone-13`; added by the user 2026-10-05): the game check** — branch `milestone-13`, spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-05-milestone-13-game-check-design.md` (approved by the user 2026-10-05), plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-05-milestone-13-game-check.md` (8 tasks; fix tasks added after Task 7) — brainstormed 2026-10-05: the user asked to "finish emulation features" and add RetroAchievements → two milestones, **13 the game check** first, **14 RetroAchievements** after. Claude judged none of the known gaps (mid-line drawing, the built-in BIOS's sound calls, green swap / DMA3 video capture, the last mGBA timing tests) clearly worth it for v1 alone, so the user chose a game check + fixes: the user's games plus ~20–30 free homebrew games (Claude asks once before downloading), judged **automatically only — no user check** — by contact-sheet pictures plus a warning log of anything unsupported (in a new `build/check` PC build only; the app is unchanged); small fixes made directly, a big one shown to the user first; each passing game locked in (`dev.sh games --check`); nothing may break (CTest, mGBA suite scores, PC speed ≥ 97 %, old save states keep loading). Done when every game runs its script with no crash or freeze, looks right to Claude, every log line is fixed or explained, and all games are locked in.
- **Milestone 14 (done, tag `milestone-14`; added by the user 2026-10-05): RetroAchievements** — branch `milestone-14`, spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-06-milestone-14-retroachievements-design.md` (brainstormed and approved 2026-10-06; refined while planning: the hardcore limits apply only while signed in **and the game has a set**; games are identified before they start), plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-06-milestone-14-retroachievements.md` (13 tasks). User decisions: the **one exception** to the "no third-party sign-ins" rule — the user's own retroachievements.org sign-in (token kept in `files/ra.json`, never the password, kept out of backups) through rcheevos v12.5.0 (MIT; committed in `third_party/rcheevos/` by Task 1 — download allowed by the user 2026-10-06), no app key; **core set**: sign in, unlock pop-ups, per-game Achievements list in the pause menu, progress, challenge badge, mastered — **no leaderboards**; **Hardcore switch, off by default until RetroAchievements approves the app** (changed by the user 2026-10-06 during their try — the server refuses hardcore unlocks from an app it doesn't know; blocks state loading, rewind, cheats in games with a set; saving states and fast-forward allowed); **Continue in hardcore starts the game fresh**; approach 1: rcheevos in the C glue `platform/ra/`, the Kotlin app does the internet and the pop-ups. Done when all tests pass, Claude's emulator check with the pretend server passes (impeccable review `ship`), and **the user signs in and unlocks a real achievement** on the phone emulator. Hardcore credit needs RetroAchievements to approve the app by its User-Agent (the user, when the app goes public; softcore until then).
- **Milestone 15 (done, tag `milestone-15`; added by the user 2026-10-06): RetroAchievements leaderboards** — branch `milestone-15` (merged), spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-06-milestone-15-leaderboards-design.md` (brainstormed 2026-10-06, spec approved by the user 2026-10-06), plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-06-milestone-15-leaderboards.md` (9 tasks). **The catch:** rcheevos runs leaderboards only in hardcore, and RetroAchievements refuses hardcore from the app until it approves it — so real scores can't be sent yet; reading boards works. User decisions: the **full set** (a Leaderboards page in the pause menu — its own row under Achievements — plus the live parts: started / failed notes, live counter pills, the submitted score with its rank, tested with the pretend servers); a board shows the **top 10 + a few rows around the user**, no profile pictures; a Settings switch **"Leaderboard notes on screen"** (on by default; hides the counter and notes, not the score card); built through rcheevos in the existing glue (approach 1). Done when all tests pass, Claude's emulator check with the pretend server passes (impeccable `ship`), and **the user opens a real game's Leaderboards** on the phone emulator and sees the real scores.
- **Milestone 16 (done 2026-11-12; DreamBoy 1.0.0 public at https://github.com/tengencode/DreamBoy): release prep — DreamBoy 1.0 on GitHub** — spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-12-milestone-16-release-prep-design.md`. User decisions: free and open source, GPL-3.0-or-later; the public name **DreamBoy** (long-term goal: one app for Game Boy, Game Boy Color and GBA — the first release plays GBA only, GB/GBC added after); people find it on **GitHub or Google and sideload the APK** (no Play Store); success = stars, installs, forks, and the failure to avoid = being hard to find; the repo goes under the user's GitHub account **`tengencode`** as `DreamBoy`; the README says it was built with Claude. Claude's calls: app id `com.tengen.dreamboy` (debug builds `com.tengen.dreamboy.debug`), version 1.0.0 driving the User-Agent, an About page with licences and the privacy policy (`PRIVACY.md`), offline unlocks kept across restarts (a connection lost mid-game), a play-first README with homebrew screenshots, topics, a social preview, a launch checklist and post drafts. Not this milestone: GB/GBC, Play / F-Droid / IzzyOnDroid (IzzyOnDroid rejects AI-written apps), Google developer verification (needed only from the 2027 worldwide rollout), RetroAchievements approval (6 months after release).
- **Milestone 17 (in progress, added by the user 2026-11-12): Game Boy and Game Boy Color in the same app** — branch `milestone-17`, spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-12-milestone-17-game-boy-design.md` (brainstormed and approved by the user 2026-11-12; the test-ROM downloads allowed), 17a plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-12-milestone-17a-gb-core.md` (12 tasks). Why: high demand, roll out fast. User decisions: **our own core** (SameBoy, MIT, was offered as the faster route and declined); **two releases** — first (1.1.0): play `.gb` / `.gbc` with sound, saves and the cartridge clock, save states, rewind, fast-forward, touch buttons, console skins, keyboard, gamepad, box art, backup; next: cheats (Game Genie, GameShark), RetroAchievements, Delta skins for these consoles, colour choices for grey games, rumble and tilt cartridges; not planned: link cable, Super Game Boy. Claude's calls: one core for both consoles (`core_gb/`, target `gbcore`), no boot ROM, machine-cycle timing with a line renderer, the same picture format / sound rate (32,768 Hz) / frame rate (59.7275 Hz) as the GBA core, a thin shared "machine" layer the tools and the Android code call. Parts: **17a** the engine on the PC (done when the spec's test-ROM list passes and nothing GBA changes) → **17b** the engine in the app + release 1.1.0 (done when the user plays a Game Boy and a Game Boy Color game on their phone) → **17c** the extras (own spec).
- **Milestone 6 (done): the Kotlin app shell**, split (user decision 2026-09-28) into **6a game library** (done, tag `milestone-6a`: design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-28-milestone-6a-library-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-milestone-6a-library.md` (5 tasks) — done when the user picks their games folder on the emulator, opens game A from the list, quits and sees it on top with its picture and time, and exports their save to Download; all tests pass, the core unchanged, CTest 120/120) → **6b settings + controller remapping** (done, tag `milestone-6b`: design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-28-milestone-6b-settings-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-milestone-6b-settings.md` (6 tasks) — done when the user, in game A on the emulator, changes settings from the in-game menu and sees them take effect (smooth pixels, a size, a remapped key, the sideways look), the game settings and the BIOS choice work, and all tests pass, CTest 120/120) → **6c the touch layout editor** (done, tag `milestone-6c`: design `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-02-milestone-6c-layout-editor-design.md`, plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-02-milestone-6c-layout-editor.md` (5 tasks) — done when the user, in game A on the emulator, moves and resizes touch buttons and the game picture upright and sideways, saves and plays with the new layout, and all tests pass, CTest 120/120; button pictures and skin `.zip` import are not in v1) (sensors and rumble were done in 4d).
