## Completed
### Milestone 17b (plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-13-milestone-17b-gb-app.md`)
- **17b Task 1** — the native layer runs both consoles: `emu_thread.c` / `jni_bridge.c` on `machine_*` (GBA-only things — BIOS, forced save type, sensors, rumble, cheats, achievement memory — only when the machine is a GBA; a Game Boy game gets **no RetroAchievements session**, cheats refused, its cartridge clock told the time once a second); the picture's size travels with the frame (`tb_init(tb, w, h)`, `video_gl` with a `u_size` uniform, `fit_rect(…, frame_w, frame_h)`, readback); JNI `loadGame(rom, savePath, console)`, `console()`, `frameWidth()`, `frameHeight()`, `ERR_UNSUPPORTED = 100`; a ROM with no usable header and no hint is still taken for GBA (the tiny test ROMs). Own demo ROMs from bytes: `tools/build_gb_test_roms.py` → `tests/own_roms/gb_demo.gb`, `gbc_demo.gbc` (scrolling stripes, a tone, a counter in cartridge RAM), fingerprints with sound locked. **A gap closed:** rewinding lost the frame's unread sound (the GBA core keeps it in its state) — the Game Boy state now has a `SND ` chunk too. **A miss from 17a found:** the on-device fingerprint tests still expected 32 entries and lacked the Game Boy files — they were never run in 17a; now 38 entries, the acid and two blargg ROMs packed into the test assets. `GameBoyDeviceTest` (12 tests). Unit 602, CTest 276, Kotlin 269, on-device 105.
- **17b Task 2** — reading game files: `Console.kt` (GBA / GB / GBC with the picture size, the L / R flag, the label mark and the layout family; `Console.of(rom, fileName)` is the C side's `machine_detect` rule again — content first, the name's ending only without a usable header), `RomFile` takes `.gb` / `.gbc` (also as the first game file in a zip; `readNamed` gives the zip's inner name, `zipGameName`), `Library` lists and names them. Tests: `ConsoleTest` (3), 1 more in `RomFileTest`, more cases in `LibraryTest`.
### Milestone 17a (plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-12-milestone-17a-gb-core.md`)
- **17a Task 1** — the library skeleton and cartridge header: `core_gb/` (target `gbcore`), `gb.h` / `gb_debug.h`, ROM loading (size checks, chip table, RAM size, title, CRC-32, the colour flag), `tests/unit/gbtestutil` + 10 tests. Deviations: RAM code 1 = 2 KiB (homebrew uses it; the plan said 0); a refused ROM leaves the loaded one in place; internal names carry a `gb_` prefix (a link clash with the GBA core's `cart_load`). Unit 495.
- **17a Task 2** — the SM83 CPU (`cpu.c`: decoded by bit fields, time passing through the bus — every read, write and internal cycle calls `gb_tick`), a first memory map (`bus.c`: ROM flat, work RAM + echo, high RAM, IF / IE), `gb_reset` / `gb_run_frame` / the debug calls; 21 tests (every opcode's ticks, the ALU and all 256 CB opcodes against models in the tests, DAA over BCD sums, interrupts, EI delay, HALT and its bug, the push-onto-IE cancel, frames never drift or hang). Note: the code was written before its tests here (they passed first time). STOP idles like HALT until Task 8's speed switch. Unit 516.
- **17a Task 3** — the divider and timer as a falling-edge counter on one divider bit (`timer.c`: DIV / TAC writes can count once, TIMA shows 0 for a cycle before TMA loads, writes in those two cycles cancel or are lost), the serial port with no cable clocked from the divider's bit 8, the joypad and its interrupt (`serial.c`), the serial bytes kept for tests (`gb_debug_serial`); 9 tests. Unit 525.
- **17a Task 4** — cartridge chips MBC1 (the 0x20-bank quirk, mode 1), MBC2 (512 half-bytes), MBC3 (and the bigger MBC30 variant: 8 bank bits past 2 MiB, 8 RAM banks), MBC5 (9-bit bank, rumble bit ignored), RAM without a chip, the dirty flag; bank offsets wrapped to the real ROM / RAM sizes so a lying header cannot reach outside (a test hammers every register on 48 lying ROMs under the sanitizer); 8 tests. MBC3 clock registers read 0 until Task 9. Unit 533.
- **17a Task 5** — the machine layer (`machine/`: one handle over `gba_*` / `gb_*`; a ROM's console found by content first — a GBA header's fixed byte + checksum, else a Game Boy header checksum with a size byte that fits the file — then by the file name), the test runner on it (every GBA test and fingerprint unchanged; `frame_hash` takes a pixel count; new `--gb-serial` / `--gb-memtext` / `--gb-mooneye`), `tools/fetch_test_roms.py` fetches blargg's ROMs (retrio/gb-test-roms at a pinned commit, pinned by the SHA-256 of its 58 ROMs' hashes), the mooneye package `mts-20240926-1737-443f6e1` and dmg-acid2 / cgb-acid2 with their reference pictures (all pinned, each downloaded twice), `tests/gb_tests.json` → CTest `gb_*`. **72 test ROMs pass and are locked in:** blargg cpu_instrs 01-11 and instr_timing, mem_timing 01-03 (both sets); mooneye bits (2), daa, timer (12 of 13), ie_push, 9 root timing tests, every MBC1 / MBC2 / MBC5 test but the multi-game one, boot_regs-dmgABC. Still open (they wait on the screen's LY, Task 6): the combined cpu_instrs / mem_timing ROMs, halt_bug, most root timing tests, oam_dma, boot_div / boot_hwio; `timer/rapid_toggle` fails (to look at with Task 6). Deviation: Game Boy detection uses no logo bytes (none of Nintendo's data in the source). The Game Boy's sound, clock footer, states and rewind are stubs behind the layer until Tasks 7, 9, 10. A Bash-tool trap found: heredocs holding an odd number of apostrophes fail to parse — write such scripts with the Write tool. Unit 539, CTest 195.
- **17a Task 6** — the screen: `ppu.c` (timing as hardware tests measure it — per line: dot 0 the new LY, OAM busy and the OAM interrupt source; dot 4 STAT shows mode 2 and LY is compared with LYC; dot 80 video RAM busy; dot 84 STAT shows mode 3; dot 252 + scroll / sprite / window extras the line is drawn and the HBlank source rises; 4 dots later STAT shows mode 0 and memory is free; the STAT line with its one-at-a-time rule, kept while the screen is off; the first line after switching on; LY 153 for 4 dots; OAM DMA with its 2-cycle start; the original Game Boy's STAT-write interrupt quirk), `ppu_draw.c` (a whole line at a time: background, window with its own line counter, 10 sprites a line with the x-then-OAM-order priority), a back buffer shown when VBlank starts (frames are a fixed 70,224 ticks, so the picture never tears when a game restarts the screen). **dmg-acid2 matches its reference picture exactly** (`tools/png_compare.py`, new; hash locked in `expected_frames.json`). mooneye `acceptance/ppu`: 10 of 12 pass (hblank_ly_scx_timing-GS, intr_1_2_timing-GS, intr_2_0_timing, intr_2_mode0_timing, intr_2_mode3_timing, intr_2_oam_ok_timing, lcdon_timing-GS, stat_irq_blocking, stat_lyc_onoff, vblank_stat_intr-GS); **not passing, as the spec allows for a line renderer:** intr_2_mode0_timing_sprites (exact per-sprite drawing time), lcdon_write_timing-GS (OAM / video RAM writes right after switching on). Also now passing: all 13 timer tests (a TIMA wrap caused by a TAC write reloads in the same cycle — found with rapid_toggle), oam_dma (all), boot_div (the divider starts at 0xABC8 in this core's tick-before-access scheme = the hardware's 0xABCC at the first fetch), every root timing test. **110 test ROMs locked in.** Bugs the tests caught: a blank screen was 0xFFFF, not white 0x7FFF. Still open: boot_hwio (needs the sound registers, Task 7); the combined cpu_instrs / mem_timing / halt_bug ROMs carry the colour flag and switch speed (Task 8). Deviations from the plan: OAM DMA blocks only OAM for the CPU (not everything but high RAM); `state_` / `rewind_` CTest variants are skipped for `gb/` entries until Task 10. Unit 563, CTest 234.
- **17a Task 7** — sound (`apu.c`): the two squares (sweep with its at-once overflow check and the leave-down rule), the wave channel, noise (15 / 7-bit shift register), the 512 Hz sequencer clocked by the divider's bit 12 (a divider reset can clock it), length counters with the first-half extra clock, envelopes, converters, power off / on (the original Game Boy keeps its length counters), routing and volume; integer maths only; each 32,768 Hz sample is the mix averaged over its 128 ticks, through an integer DC filter; a 2048-sample ring for `gb_audio_read`. The boot state (registers, a faded chime on channel 1, P1 select 0, a VBlank request left in IF, the screen in line 153 dot 400) now passes mooneye **boot_hwio-dmgABCmgb**. blargg **dmg_sound: 9 of 12** (01 registers, 02 len ctr, 03 trigger, 04 sweep, 05 sweep details, 06 overflow on trigger, 07 len sweep period sync, 08 len ctr during power, 11 regs after power); **not passing** (time-boxed, as planned): 09 wave read while on, 10 wave trigger while on, 12 wave write while on — all need the wave channel timed inside a machine cycle. 14 unit tests (pitches by counting cycles: 524 Hz squares, a 262 Hz wave; silence exactly 0). Deviation: no own sound ROM / audio fingerprint here — the pitch tests cover it and the homebrew games of Task 12 lock audio hashes. The tests were again written after the code (2 wrong expectations of mine fixed, not the code). **131 test ROMs locked in** (some cgb_sound ones already pass too). Unit 577, CTest 255.
- **17a Task 8** — Game Boy Color (`cgb.c`, `ppu_draw.c` for both consoles): double speed through STOP (a machine cycle is 2 clock ticks; the CPU, timer and OAM DMA run twice as fast against the screen and sound; the sequencer follows divider bit 13), 8 work-RAM banks, 2 video-RAM banks, palette RAM with its moving index (shut while a line is drawn), HDMA all-at-once and one-block-a-line (the CPU stands still through a stall counter), the undocumented registers; drawing with tile attributes (palette, bank, flips, priority), sprites by OAM order (or the old rule on request), LCDC bit 0 as master priority. **cgb-acid2 matches its reference picture exactly** (hash locked). Now passing too: the combined blargg cpu_instrs, mem_timing and mem_timing-2 ROMs (they carry the colour flag and switch speed), halt_bug and interrupt_time (they report on screen only: fingerprints locked after reading "Passed" on the picture), mooneye serial/boot_sclk_align (the serial clock is one cycle ahead of divider bit 8). blargg **cgb_sound: 11 of 12** (all but 10 wave trigger while on). **Out of scope by the spec** (they test a Game Boy Color running a plain Game Boy game in its compatibility mode; this core runs such games as an original Game Boy): mooneye misc boot_regs-cgb, boot_div-cgbABCDE, boot_hwio-C, bits/unused_hwio-C, ppu/vblank_stat_intr-C. Also not passing and not aimed for: blargg oam_bug (the original Game Boy's OAM corruption bug). **135 test ROMs + 4 picture fingerprints locked in.** 9 unit tests (written after the code). Unit 586, CTest 262.
- **17a Task 9** — the cartridge clock and the save file (`rtc.c`): the MBC3 clock's registers belong to the game (set, halted, read through the latch); `gb_set_rtc` only says what time it is and the registers move on by the real seconds since the last call (worked out, not counted; backwards or absurd jumps add nothing; the day counter's carry bit); the save file = cartridge RAM + the 48-byte footer other emulators use (a 44-byte one is read too), built and read by the core; a save of any length loads (what fits is copied, the rest 0xFF, a cut footer ignored); the clock runs through a reset, starts at 0 for a new cartridge. 7 tests, written first — the sanitizer caught a signed overflow with nonsense times (fixed: unsigned difference, 100-year cap). The pinned mooneye package has no MBC3 tests. Unit 593, CTest 262.
- **17a Task 10** — save states and rewind. The rewind history moved out of the GBA core into `core_common/` (`rewind_ring.c` — the ring without any core in it — and `delta_pack.c`; target `corecommon`, linked by both cores and added to the Android CMake); `core/src/rewind.c` is now a thin wrapper; every GBA state / rewind test and the old-state guard passed unchanged before anything else was touched. `core_gb/src/state.c`: magic `GBCS`, 12 versioned chunks (CPU, MEM, TMR, SER, JOY, CART, PPU, APU, CGB, PICT = both pictures, CRAM = the cartridge RAM, MISC), everything checked before anything changes; values that index memory or keep a loop going are range-checked and the cartridge's bank offsets are worked out again, never taken from the file; rewind re-made with the new state size when another ROM is loaded. The machine layer forwards both; the test runner's `--state-at` / `--rewind-at` work for Game Boy ROMs, so the 4 Game Boy fingerprints now also run through a state and through a rewind in CTest. 6 tests written first (round trip for both consoles, refusals, every chunk filled with 0x00 / 0xFF under the sanitizer, rewind, a GBA state into a Game Boy machine and back). Unit 599, CTest 270.
- **17a Task 11** — the viewer and the speed bench on the machine layer (`gbaview` opens .gba / .gb / .gbc, its window sized to the picture; `gbabench` runs scenes of both consoles; GBA scene hashes unchanged), 3 Game Boy speed scenes from free homebrew games (`tests/bench/scenes.txt`: gb_ucity — Game Boy Color, double speed —, gb_geometrix, gb_libbet; the bench finds `homebrew/` files in `tests/roms/`). **A real bug found by the bench:** a finished OAM DMA leaves its counter at 0xA0, which the state check refused — so any game using OAM DMA could not load a state or rewind (fixed, test `gb_state_after_oam_dma`). Also fixed after the automated security review of Task 10: a loaded state's sound timers and mixer sums are range-checked (own commit). **Speed (PC, `bench --runs 5`, 2026-11-12):** first measured gb_ucity 1797 fps (30×), gb_geometrix 2818 (47×), gb_libbet 2494 (42×) — far under the spec's stand-in gate (5× the slowest GBA scene, game_b 1137 fps → 5685). Sped up without changing any result: sound worked in batches between events (`gb_apu_sync`), a tile row fetched once per tile, link-time optimisation for `gbcore` in Release builds, the idle (HALT) cycles in one loop, a timer fast path. **Now gb_ucity 4189 fps (70×), gb_geometrix 5634 (94×), gb_libbet 3907 (65×)** = 3.4-4.9× game_b: **the stand-in gate (5×) is still missed.** The real goal is ≥ 4× on phone B's small core: by the GBA core's C-only PC-to-phone ratio in milestone 7a (about 13) that is roughly 5-7× there — to be **measured on the phone at the start of 17b** (`dev.sh phone-bench`, needs the user's phone); if it misses, the next step is batching machine cycles between events (a scheduler). GBA scenes within their floors (game_a 1362, game_b 1137, game_c 2093, game_d 1151, m7_demo 2551, snd1_demo 6921). Unit 600, CTest 270.
- **17a Task 12** — the game check with homebrew games; 17a exit. `tools/game_check.py` takes .gb / .gbc (and a zip holding one), refuses L / R / CW / CCW in a Game Boy game's script, passes `--notes` only for GBA; 5 free homebrew games pinned in `fetch_test_roms.py` and locked in `tests/games/games.txt`: gb_ucity (Game Boy Color, GPL-3), gb_geometrix (Color, GPL-3), gb_libbet (Color-enhanced, zlib), gb_shock_lobster (plain Game Boy, zlib, in a zip), gb_240p (test tool, GPL-2). Claude looked at every contact sheet (colours, menus, play, pause screens right) and probed the sound files. **`games --check`: 36/36** (the 31 GBA entries unchanged). Deviation: 5 games, not the planned 6-8 with 3 + 3 (few homebrew projects publish stable release files; only one is a plain Game Boy game) — more belong in 17b's check, with the user's own games in `games.local.txt`. ponytail-review of the branch: 3 findings, all fixed (a compile-time check that the two pictures stay neighbours for the PICT chunk; a test for the original Game Boy's STAT-write quirk; the Android debug app built with `dev.sh apk` — it still builds). Not merged into `main`: 17b continues on `milestone-17`. Unit 601, CTest 270, tool tests 18, `games --check` 36/36, `bench --check` ok.
- `8eef1b9` — design spec written and approved.
- `3a1a702` — milestone 1 plan (21 tasks, full code in each step).
- `8ee77fd` — `CLAUDE.md` + this file (working rules and handoff).
- **Task 1** — llvm-mingw installed via winget (clang 22); CMake/Ninja build, `tools/dev.sh`, C test harness, smoke test passes **with sanitizers ON** (ASan+UBSan work on llvm-mingw, no fallback needed).
- **Task 2** — event scheduler (`core/src/sys/sched.{h,c}`), 4 tests pass. Also added `.gitattributes` forcing LF endings (Git here converts to CRLF, which breaks `tools/dev.sh`).
- **Task 3** — cartridge ROM loading, header title/code, ROM reads incl. past-the-end pattern (`core/src/cart`). Plan bug fixed: test used `0x0D000010` as a ROM mirror; correct mirror is `0x0C000010` (code was right).
- **Task 4** — memory map (mirrors, VRAM/palette/OAM byte-write rules, BIOS read protection, open bus, wait states), I/O registers (DISPCNT/DISPSTAT/VCOUNT/KEYINPUT/IE/IF/WAITCNT/IME/POSTFLG/HALTCNT), interrupt controller. 28 tests pass, no warnings.
- **Task 5** — LCD line timing: HBlank/VBlank/VCount flags + IRQs, VCOUNT wraps at 228, `frame_done` at line 160, `gba_run_events` dispatcher. 31 tests pass.
- **Task 6** — CPU core: registers, mode banking, exceptions (UND/SWI/IRQ entry), condition codes, `cpu_step` (ARM+Thumb fetch), `gba_run_frame` with halt handling, HLE boot state (SPs, SYS mode, PC 0x08000000), real-BIOS path. ARM/Thumb decoders are skeletons (everything "undefined") until Tasks 8-15. Test helpers `tests/unit/testutil.{h,c}`. 41 tests pass.
- **Task 7** — ALU helpers in `core/src/cpu/alu.h`: adder with carry/overflow, barrel shifter (immediate + register forms incl. special cases), multiplier timing. 45 tests pass.
- **Task 8** — ARM data processing (all 16 ops, immediate/shifted operands, PC+12 on register shifts, S-bit with PC = exception return, 'bad CMP' quirk) and MRS/MSR (user mode flag-only, T bit protected). 57 tests pass.
- **Task 9** — ARM B/BL/BX and SWI; the frame loop now runs real CPU code (`b .` spin test) and wakes a halted CPU on an enabled IRQ. 65 tests pass.
- **Task 10** — ARM MUL/MLA and UMULL/UMLAL/SMULL/SMLAL with multiplier timing; flags set N/Z only (C/V kept). 67 tests pass.
- **Task 11** — ARM LDR/STR(B) with all offset/index modes, LDRH/STRH/LDRSB/LDRSH (rotated/misaligned rules), SWP/SWPB; shared load helpers in `core/src/cpu/cpu_mem.h`. 74 tests pass.
- **Task 12** — ARM LDM/STM: all addressing modes, writeback, empty-list quirk, base-in-list rules, user-bank (^) transfers, LDM {pc}^ exception return. **ARM instruction set complete.** 83 tests pass.
- **Task 13** — Thumb formats 1-5: immediate shifts, add/sub, 8-bit immediates, the 16 ALU ops, high-register ADD/CMP/MOV and BX. 91 tests pass.
- **Task 14** — Thumb formats 6-15: PC-relative load, register/immediate-offset loads & stores (byte/half/signed), SP-relative, ADD PC/SP, SP adjust, PUSH/POP, LDMIA/STMIA with empty-list and base-in-list quirks. 100 tests pass.
- **Task 15** — Thumb formats 16-19: conditional branch, SWI, unconditional branch, two-half BL. **Thumb instruction set complete — whole CPU done.** 104 tests pass.
- **Task 16** — HLE BIOS math in `core/src/bios/hle_math.c`: Div (incl. /0 and INT_MIN/-1), Sqrt, ArcTan (BIOS polynomial), ArcTan2, BgAffineSet, ObjAffineSet (256-entry sine table). Declarations for all HLE functions in `hle_internal.h`. 110 tests pass.
- **Task 17** — HLE BIOS memory SWIs in `core/src/bios/hle_memory.c`: CpuSet (copy/fill, 16/32-bit, refuses BIOS source), CpuFastSet (rounds to 8 words), BitUnPack. 115 tests pass.
- **Task 18** — HLE BIOS decompression in `core/src/bios/hle_decompress.c`: LZ77 (4 KiB window), Huffman (4/8-bit), run-length, Diff8/Diff16; VRAM variants write halfwords. Plan bug fixed: test `hle_diff16` clashed with the function name → renamed `hle_diff16_halfwords`. 121 tests pass.
- **Task 19** — HLE BIOS stub (`core/src/bios/bios_stub.S` → generated `bios_stub.inc`, 612 bytes) with real-BIOS-matching IRQ handler, SWI entry/exit, IntrWait/VBlankIntrWait in ARM; everything else traps to C via `0xE7F000F0` (`hle_swi`). Boot latch 0xE129F000, SoftReset, RegisterRamReset, Halt/Stop, checksum, SoundBias. End-to-end tests: ARM + Thumb SWI Div round trip, VBlankIntrWait running a game ISR. 130 tests pass.
- **Task 20** — debug API (`core/include/gba_debug.h`), headless `tools/testrunner` (idle-loop detection, `--result-reg`, exit 77 = ROM missing), `tools/fetch_test_roms.py` (pinned commit, size-checked), CTest entries `jsmolka_{arm,thumb,memory,bios}` (skipped until ROMs are fetched). 131 unit tests pass.
- **Task 21** — **Exit gate passed on the first run:** jsmolka `arm`, `thumb`, `memory`, `bios` all PASS (each finishes in 2 frames); CTest 5/5; ASan+UBSan clean. Runner verified to report failures (pointing it at r13 prints FAIL). No fixes needed.

- **M2 Task 1** — framebuffer (`gba_framebuffer()`), each visible line drawn at HBlank, layer line buffers (`core/src/ppu/ppu_internal.h`), priority compositor (`ppu_compose.c`), forced blank = white. Test helpers `tu_io16/tu_pal/tu_vram16/tu_oam16/tu_px/...` added. 135 tests pass. No surprises.

- **M2 Task 2** — text (tiled) backgrounds in `core/src/ppu/ppu_bg.c`: 4bpp/8bpp, palette banks, scroll + wrap, H/V flips, 512-wide/tall maps, priority, mosaic. 142 tests pass.

- **M2 Task 3** — affine (rotate/scale) backgrounds: BG2 in mode 1, BG2+BG3 in mode 2, wrap flag, mosaic; internal reference points `ref_x/ref_y` reload on BGxX/Y writes (`io.c` hook), step by PB/PD per line, relatch at VBlank; HLE boot and RegisterRamReset set PA=PD=1.0. 149 tests pass.

- **M2 Task 4** — bitmap modes 3/4/5 (BG2 through the affine path; mode 4 index 0 transparent; page select at 0xA000; mode 5 is 160x128). **Plan bug fixed:** two tests drew lines 1-2 directly with `ppu_render_line`, but bitmap/affine BGs sample through the internal reference, which only advances as a frame runs — tests now set BG2Y to the line first (plan updated). Remember this for later scene tests on lines > 0 with affine/bitmap BGs. 153 tests pass.

- **M2 Task 5** — sprites in `core/src/ppu/ppu_obj.c`: regular + affine + double-size, 4/8bpp, 1D/2D mapping, flips, position wrap, OAM-order ties, bitmap-mode tile < 512 hidden, per-line cycle budget (1210/954), OBJ window mask, semi-transparent flag, OBJ mosaic. 166 tests pass. No surprises.

- **M2 Task 6** — compositor (`ppu_compose.c`): WIN0 > WIN1 > OBJ window > outside, wrapping spans, per-window effect bit; alpha / brighten / darken with coefficients capped at 16; backdrop as a target; semi-transparent sprites always blend with a 2nd target. **Rendering (Tasks 1-6) complete.** 175 tests pass.

- **M2 Task 7** — timers in `core/src/sys/timer.{h,c}`: lazy counting (value computed on read), prescalers 1/64/256/1024, overflow event `EV_TIMER0..3` reloads + IRQ, cascade (count-up), stop freezes the count; I/O routing for 0x100-0x10F in `io.c`. 181 tests pass. Known small gap: a byte write to a TMxCNT_H re-writes control and restarts timing from now (loses the partial prescaler tick) — fine for now.

- **M2 Task 8** — DMA in `core/src/sys/dma.{h,c}`: channels 0-3 latch src/dst/count on enable, immediate start 2 cycles later (`EV_DMA`), VBlank + HBlank (visible lines only) triggers from `ppu.c`, inc/dec/fixed/inc+reload, repeat, IRQ, disable after a non-repeat transfer, DMA latch for unreadable sources (< 0x02000000); CPU stall = bus cycles advance `g->now`. **Two plan test bugs fixed** (plan updated): the VBlank-repeat test needed a fixed source (sources are never reloaded); the HBlank test jumped a full frame in one `tu_advance_to`, so the 160 back-to-back transfers' stall time pushed the clock into the next frame's line 0 — it now stops at line 200. Watch for this in any test that advances far with DMA active. 188 unit tests + jsmolka CPU ROMs pass. Known gap: one shared `EV_DMA` event, so a second immediate channel enabled later delays the first by a few cycles.

- **M2 Task 9** — shared tool library `tools/common` (`gbatools`: PNG writer with stored deflate blocks, `read_file`, FNV-1a `frame_hash`), `tools/png_probe.py` checker, test runner gains `--frames` (exact), `--screenshot`, `--hash`, `--expect-hash`. First real picture: jsmolka `arm.gba` at 10 frames shows 'All tests passed' (hash `ffb537b2caeb46f1`). Match → exit 0, mismatch → exit 1. CTest 5/5.

- **M2 Task 11** — `gbaview.exe` Win32 viewer (`tools/viewer/main.c`, GDI StretchDIBits, 3x, paced to 59.7275 fps with QueryPerformanceCounter + timeBeginPeriod(1)); keys arrows/Z/X/A/S/Enter/Backspace, F12 screenshot, Esc quit; `--autotest <frames> <png>` self-test; `bash tools/dev.sh view <args>`. Self-test on jsmolka `hello.gba`: 120 frames in 2.00 s (60.0 fps), shows 'Hello world!'. Committed before Task 10 because the Tonc download was failing (see Notes).

- **M2 Task 10** — `tools/fetch_test_roms.py` now fetches jsmolka `hello/shades/stripes` (sizes matched the plan) and Tonc demos (see blocker in Notes; fails gracefully, jsmolka still fetched); `tools/approve_frame.py`; `tests/expected_frames.json` (empty); root `CMakeLists.txt` makes one `frame_<name>` CTest per approved entry. CTest 5/5 with the empty list. Tonc zip downloaded later the same day and SHA-256-pinned (see Notes).

- **M2 Task 12** — rendered all 7 jsmolka ROMs at 10 frames; every probed colour matched the plan (stripes #5A84AD/#84A5C6, shades #000000→#0000E7, hello black, CPU ROMs white) and Claude inspected each PNG (hello: "Hello world!" centred; shades: 16 blue bands; stripes: even two-tone stripes; arm/thumb/memory/bios: identical "All tests passed" frame, hash `ffb537b2caeb46f1`). Approved into `tests/expected_frames.json`. CTest 12/12 pass.

- **M2 Task 13** — rendered all 19 Tonc demos at 60 frames (no timeouts, crashes or sanitizer reports) and compared each with the guide's picture in the browser pane (gbadev.net/tonc). **One real bug found and fixed** (`11db3ff`): the frame loop cached the next event time for a whole CPU batch, so an immediate DMA waited; tonclib's next `dma_cpy` cleared the channel first and the copy was lost (`dma_demo` showed a black circle instead of the Brinstar map). Now the loop re-reads the next event after every instruction and immediate DMA runs right after the enabling instruction (2-cycle delay charged as idle time); spec §4 updated; new unit test `dma_immediate_runs_before_the_cpu_moves_on`. Differences that are **not** bugs (checked against the demo sources): `tmr_demo` stays 00:00:00 because the demo never enables timer 2 until Start is pressed; `oacombo`, `sbb_reg`, `sbb_aff`, `bld_demo`, `win_demo`, `mos_demo`, `obj_aff` are key-driven, so the guide shows them after input; `swi_demo` labels match the source (the guide's picture is from an older build) and its atan curve is right-half only because the real BIOS returns 0..FFFFh; `cbb_demo` matches the *hardware* picture; `irq_demo` gradient and red band (lines 80-119) match the guide exactly. All 19 approved (`tonc_*`, 60 frames). CTest 31/31.

- **M2 Task 14** — exit gate: CTest 31/31 (unit tests + 4 jsmolka CPU ROMs + 7 jsmolka frames + 19 Tonc frames), 189 unit tests, ASan + UBSan clean. **User checked `gbaview` live (2026-09-25):** `obj_demo` runs smoothly, arrow keys move the game L, F12 saved `gbaview-0.png`, Esc closes; `tmr_demo` clock starts ticking on Enter (Start). **Milestone 2 complete** — tag `milestone-2`, merged into `main` (user choice), branch deleted, CTest 31/31 on `main`.

- **M3 brainstorming (2026-09-25)** — user decisions: split milestone 3 into 3a sound → 3b saves → 3c timing; gbaview plays sound (waveOut, audio-paced); DMA-sound testing uses **our own assembly test ROMs** (committed, built with NDK clang) plus Tonc `snd1_demo`; sampling at the hardware rate (one stereo sample per 512 cycles, 32,768 Hz). Mixer constants checked against mGBA (`src/gba/audio.c`, `src/gb/audio.c`) and GBATEK. Design written: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-25-milestone-3a-sound-design.md`; parent spec milestone table updated.

- **M3a Task 1** — APU core (`core/src/apu/apu.{h,c}`): sound registers 0x060-0x0A7 routed from `io.c` (byte writes to FIFOs queue one byte), SOUNDCNT_X master switch (off clears+locks 0x060-0x081), SOUNDBIAS reset 0x200, FIFO A/B (32 bytes, full-drop, empty-hold, reset bits), timer 0/1 overflow pops (hook in `timer.c`), mixer `apu_mix_side` (checked against mGBA formula), `EV_APU_SAMPLE` every 512 cycles → 2048-frame ring, `gba_audio_read()`, `EV_APU_FRAME` 512 Hz; HLE RegisterRamReset bit 6 → `apu_reset`. `psg.c` is a silent stand-in (Task 2 replaces it). 204 unit tests, CTest 31/31. No surprises.

- **M3a Task 2** — PSG (`core/src/apu/psg.c`): square 1/2 (duty patterns, 16x(2048-f) cycles per step, sweep with Game Boy overflow rules), wave (bank rule, 64-sample mode, volume codes incl. forced 75%), noise (15/7-bit LFSR, 64·r·2^s period), frame sequencer (length on even steps, sweep on 2/6, envelope on 7), trigger/DAC-off rules, catch-up before register writes. All 15 PSG tests passed first time. 219 unit tests.

- **M3a Task 3** — DMA sound refill: `dma_sound_request()` in `core/src/sys/dma.c` (DMA1/DMA2 with special timing and a matching FIFO destination copy 4 words, 32-bit, fixed destination; IRQ; disable without repeat); `dma.c` refactored into `copy()`/`disable()` helpers with identical timing (all 26 approved frames unchanged); FIFO pop requests a refill at ≤16 bytes. 223 unit tests, CTest 31/31, sanitizers clean. **Batch 1 (Tasks 1-3) done.**

- **M3a Task 4** — WAV writer `tools/common/wav.{h,c}`, `audio_hash_update` (FNV-1a over int16 LE) in `frame_hash.h`, test runner drains sound every frame: `--wav`, `--audio-hash` (prints `AUDIO <hex>`), `--expect-audio-hash` (match exit 0, mismatch exit 1); CTest frame entries pass `--expect-audio-hash` when the JSON has `audio`; `approve_frame.py` takes an optional 5th audio-hash argument. Checked: Tonc `first` 60 frames → WAV `2 32768 2 32753` exactly as planned; frame hash unchanged. CTest 31/31.

- **M3a Task 5** — `tools/wav_probe.py` (pure Python: radix-2 FFT + Hann window + parabolic peak, RMS per window, 800x200 greyscale waveform PNG). Checked on a synthetic 440 Hz sine: every window reads `peak 440.0 Hz`, rms 7070.6 (expected 7071); PNG shows the tone band on the left lane and silence on the right.

- **M3a Task 6** — own sound test ROMs: `tools/build_test_roms.py` (NDK clang, header + checksum filled, logo blank) builds `tests/own_roms/src/{snd_psg,snd_dma,snd_mix}.S` (+ `common.inc` macros) into committed `tests/own_roms/*.gba` (`.gitignore` exception). All built and ran first time. Measured: snd_psg 439.9 / 879.8 / 439.9 Hz, noise rms ~10,800, sweep 126.7→140.9 Hz rising; snd_dma 512.0 Hz rms 18,979 on A then B; snd_mix L-only / R-only / 50% / 25% = 10,896 / 10,896 / 5,448 / 2,724 and DMA 50% / 100% = 9,490 / 18,979 — all within the plan's limits; waveform PNGs inspected. Current fingerprints (not approved yet — Task 9): snd_psg frame 80a69197c1fb9325 audio 01494c669155c835; snd_dma audio d524d1367567b3c5; snd_mix audio e68189d895caabc5. **Plan fixes:** snd_psg's last note ends ~5.02 s, not 5.0 s (plan text corrected); `wav_probe.py` now clamps the peak interpolation to ±0.5 bin (a mostly-silent window printed -5.8 Hz; plan code updated too). **Batch 2 (Tasks 4-6) done.**

- **M3a Task 7** — `gbaview` plays sound through waveOut (8 buffers x up to 1024 stereo frames, 32,768 Hz) and is paced by the sound card (waits while 3+ buffers are queued); `--mute` or no audio device → the old 59.7275 fps timer. Checked: muted hello 120 frames = 59.9 fps; with sound (snd_dma) 120 frames = 55.7 fps because opening the device adds a fixed ~0.1 s start-up delay — 600 frames = 10.12 s vs 10.05 s of audio (59.3 fps), so the steady rate is right. **Plan fix:** the plan expected 58-61 fps for the 120-frame sound run; corrected to explain the start-up delay.

- **M3a Task 8** — Tonc `snd1_demo` added to `TONC_DEMOS` and downloaded (zip SHA-256 check passed, first try). At 150 frames it plays SOS: D4 293.1-294.9 Hz, F4 349.7-350.1 Hz, D5 587.6 Hz, in order, twice; the envelope decays and the final D5 fades out by ~2.45 s; screen lists the notes (D+0 F+0 D+1 ...). Current fingerprints (not approved yet): frame 304f3022df934089, audio ab8eb8ad9d5ad615. **Batch 3 (Tasks 7-8) done.**

- **M3a Task 9** — **User listened in `gbaview` (2026-09-25) and confirmed all four:** snd_psg (beep, higher beep, softer sine beep, hiss, rising tone), snd_dma (two identical buzzy tones), snd_mix (left, right, quieter twice, two buzzes with the second louder), snd1_demo (SOS twice; arrow keys/Z play notes). Approved frame + audio fingerprints: `own_snd_psg` (320 frames, audio 01494c669155c835), `own_snd_dma` (130, d524d1367567b3c5), `own_snd_mix` (190, e68189d895caabc5), `tonc_snd1_demo` (150, frame 304f3022df934089, audio ab8eb8ad9d5ad615). CTest 35/35; verified CTest passes `--expect-audio-hash`. (To close a viewer window started in the background, Claude used `Stop-Process`; the resulting 'exit code 127' notices are harmless.)

- **M3a Task 10** — exit gate: CTest 35/35 (unit tests + 4 jsmolka CPU ROMs + 26 approved frames + 4 frame+audio sound tests), 223 unit tests, ASan + UBSan clean; user confirmed the sound by ear. **Milestone 3a complete** — tag `milestone-3a`, fast-forward merged into `main` (user choice), branch deleted, CTest 35/35 on `main`.

- **M3b brainstorming (2026-09-25)** — user decisions: 3b = save chips only; RTC/solar/gyro/rumble/tilt/game database move to a separate part **3b-2**; EEPROM tested with **our own assembly ROMs** (no free EEPROM test exists); `gbaview` keeps `<rom>.sav` next to the ROM; games without a marker string get **detect-on-first-use** (mGBA-style); jsmolka save ROMs download approved. Facts learned from the jsmolka `save/*.asm` sources: results in r12 (0 = pass); 16/32-bit reads replicate the byte; 16/32-bit writes store `value >> 8*(addr & 1 or 3)` at the unaligned address (our bus currently aligns — must bypass for 0x0E/0x0F); mirrors every 64 KiB and at 0x0F; flash64 contains both `FLASH_V` and `FLASH512_V`. Design written: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-25-milestone-3b-saves-design.md`; parent spec milestone table updated.

- **M3b Task 1** — save API in `gba.h` (`gba_save_type_t`, `gba_save_type/saveram/saveram_size/load_saveram/saveram_dirty/set_save_type`, `GBA_ERR_SAVE_SIZE`), `core/src/cart/save.{h,c}` (`save_t` with a fixed 128 KiB buffer inside `cart_t`; marker detection at load; UNKNOWN → SRAM on first write), SRAM (32 KiB, repeats, 0x0F mirror), bus: 0x0E/0x0F keep exact addresses, wide reads repeat the byte, wide writes store one byte by address; `gba_reset` keeps the save. Test helper `tu_rom_markers`. 233 unit tests, CTest 35/35.

- **M3b Task 2** — Flash in `core/src/cart/flash.c`: command prefix state machine, ID mode (Panasonic 0x32/0x1B, Sanyo 0x62/0x13), byte write (0xA0), chip erase (0x80+0x10), 4 KiB sector erase (0x80+0x30), bank switch (0xB0, 128K only), broken sequences start over; UNKNOWN → Flash 64K when the first write is 0xAA to 0x5555. All 7 Flash tests passed first time. 240 unit tests.

- **M3b Task 3** — EEPROM in `core/src/cart/eeprom.c`: serial protocol (1 1 = read, 1 0 = write, 6/14-bit block address, 64 data bits first byte on top, stop bit), 68-bit reply with 4 zero bits, ready bit 1 otherwise; window = all of 0x0D for ROMs ≤16 MiB, else 0x0DFFFF00+; DMA3 reports its length (9/73 → 512 B, 17/81 → 8 KiB; CPU first → 8 KiB); UNKNOWN → EEPROM on the first DMA3 into the window. All 7 EEPROM tests passed first time. 247 unit tests, CTest 35/35, sanitizers clean. **Batch 1 (Tasks 1-3) done.**

- **M3b Task 4** — jsmolka `save/sram.gba` (2084 B), `flash64.gba` (3708), `flash128.gba` (4096), `none.gba` (1628) added to `tools/fetch_test_roms.py`, downloaded (sizes matched), and run in CTest with result register r12: **all four PASS first time** (flash128 screen shows 'All tests passed'). CTest 39/39.

- **M3b Task 5** — own EEPROM test ROMs `tests/own_roms/save_eeprom512.gba` / `save_eeprom8k.gba` (sources `src/eeprom.inc` + two 4-line `.S` files): write 3 blocks by DMA3, check ready, read back, check an unwritten block is 0xFF, run marker in block 0; r12 = result; backdrop green/blue/red. Both PASS first time, full-screen green; CTest entries `own_save_eeprom512/8k` (41/41). Checked the ROM really catches errors: a planted wrong expectation gave 'FAIL test 5' and a red screen (then restored). Rebuilding also reproduced the sound ROMs byte-for-byte. **Batch 2 (Tasks 4-5) done.**

- **M3b Task 6** — `write_file()` in `tools/common/file_util`; test runner `--save <file>` loads the file before the run (wrong size → message + exit 3) and writes the chip after. Checked: two runs of `save_eeprom8k` → green then blue; the 8,192-byte file holds exactly the run marker (52554e21 0badf00d), the three patterns and an all-0xFF block 3; a 4-byte file is refused with exit 3. **Plan note:** when editing with Python inside a Bash heredoc, write `\n` escapes carefully — the tool turned `\n` into a real newline once, so match text without escapes.

- **M3b Task 7** — `gbaview` keeps `game.sav` next to the ROM: loaded at start (wrong size → warning, blank save), written 60 frames after the game last saved and on exit, atomically (`.sav.tmp` + `MoveFileExA`). Checked with `save_eeprom512` twice (`--mute --autotest 30`): green + 'saved ...' on exit, then 'loaded ...' + blue; 512-byte file, no leftover .tmp, `*.sav` gitignored.

- **M3b Task 8** — **User checked persistence in `gbaview` (2026-09-25):** `save_eeprom8k` showed green on a blank chip, wrote the 8,192-byte `tests/own_roms/save_eeprom8k.sav` on close, and showed blue on the second run (save loaded). Approved the green frames (`own_save_eeprom512`, `own_save_eeprom8k`, 10 frames, both `858a8cc4a8e35b25`). Exit gate: CTest 43/43 (unit tests + 8 jsmolka ROMs + 2 own EEPROM ROMs + 32 frame/audio fingerprints), 247 unit tests, ASan + UBSan clean. **Milestone 3b complete** — tag `milestone-3b`, fast-forward merged into `main` (user choice), branch deleted, CTest 43/43 on `main`; the viewer log confirmed `loaded ... save_eeprom8k.sav` on the blue run.

- **M3b-2 brainstorming (2026-09-25)** — user decisions: all extras in one part (RTC, solar, gyro, rumble, tilt, game list); testing by **unit tests that replay each game's documented access routine** (no free test ROMs exist); the user **has their own game A ROM** for a final real-game check (it stays on their PC, never copied into the project). Sources: GBATEK cart GPIO/RTC/solar/gyro/rumble/tilt sections and mGBA `src/gba/cart/gpio.c` (RTC bit protocol: bits in on SCK rising edge LSB-first, out on falling edge; command low nibble 6, bits 4-6 command, bit 7 read; RTC_BYTES {0,0,7,0,1,0,3,0}; control starts 0x40) and `src/gba/overrides.c` (game codes). Design written: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-25-milestone-3b2-cart-extras-design.md`.

- **M3b-2 Task 1** — `core/src/cart/hardware.{h,c}`: game list (15 code prefixes from mGBA's list, with save-type overrides), `SIIRTC_V` marker fallback, `hardware_t` inside `cart_t`, clock base (local seconds + emulated cycles; default 2000-01-01; re-based in `gba_reset` before the cycle count restarts), public API (`gba_cart_hardware/set_cart_hardware/set_rtc/set_light/set_gyro/set_tilt/rumble_active`, `GBA_HW_*`), tilt sensor at 0x0E008000-85FF routed from `save.c`. Test helper `tu_rom_code` (0xEE in ROM bytes 0xC4-0xC9). All 7 tests passed first time. 254 unit tests, CTest 43/43.

- **M3b-2 Task 2** — GPIO port in `core/src/cart/gpio.c` (data/direction/control at ROM 0xC4/C6/C8 in every ROM mirror, readable only when enabled, byte writes ignored; pins = GBA outputs + device-driven inputs), solar sensor (reset on pin 1, count on pin 0 rising edges, answer on pin 3 at 0xE8 dark .. 0x50 full sun), gyro (sample on pin 0, 4 zero bits + 12 data bits on pin 2, next bit on pin 1 falling edge; 0x6C0 still), rumble (pin 3 output + high); bus routes regions 0x08-0x0D. All 6 tests passed first time. **Plan fix:** the plan said all 6 GPIO tests would fail before the code; 2 of them check 'nothing happens' and already passed (plan text corrected). 260 unit tests, CTest 43/43.

- **M3b-2 Task 3** — real-time clock in `core/src/cart/rtc.c`: S-3511 serial protocol (CS high starts; bits in on SCK rising edge and out on falling edge, lowest first; command low nibble 6, bits 4-6 command, bit 7 read; reset / date+time / time / control; date/time writes ignored; control starts 0x40), pure calendar (Hinnant days-to-civil, weekday 0 = Sunday, 12/24-hour), hooked into the GPIO port. Tests replay game A's routines (commands 0x60/0x62/0x63/0x65/0x67 sent MSB-first): all 9 passed first time, incl. UTC offset, CS-low abort, time moving with emulated seconds and across `gba_reset`, leap day 2024, end of 2099, midnight. 269 unit tests, CTest 43/43, sanitizers clean. **Batch 1 (Tasks 1-3) done.**

- **M3b-2 Task 4** — test runner `--rtc <unix seconds>` (sets the cartridge clock, UTC) and `--hardware <rtc,solar,gyro,rumble,tilt|none>` (forces extras; unknown name → usage + exit 3). Checked: arm.gba still PASSes with both options; `--hardware sonar` → exit 3. CTest 43/43. (Usage text edit: built the backslash with `chr(92)` in Python so the shell could not turn `\n` into a newline.)

- **M3b-2 Task 5** — `gbaview`: clock set from the PC's local time at start (`GetTimeZoneInformation` for the UTC offset incl. daylight saving); keys I/J/K/L tilt (±24000), Q/E gyro (∓/±16000), [ / ] light −/+32; title shows `light <n>` for solar carts and `RUMBLE` while the motor runs. Built without warnings; muted self-test 60 frames = 59.8 fps. **Batch 2 (Tasks 4-5) done.**

- **M3b-2 Task 6** — **User's real-game check (2026-09-25)** with their game A hack `~\Downloads\hack-1.gba` (32 MiB, code BPEE, contains SIIRTC_V + FLASH1M_V; stays outside the repo): headless 600 frames fine; in `gbaview` the menu appeared **without 'The internal battery has run dry'**, controls worked. The first try was very slow with choppy sound → root cause: the viewer ran from the Debug + ASan/UBSan build (~30-45 fps on a real game) — fixed in `c8905d8` (`dev.sh view` uses an optimised `build/release`: 558 fps headless, identical frame hash, 60 fps with sound). Then the user played at full speed, saved in-game (131,072-byte Flash 128K `.sav` next to their ROM), closed, reopened: the viewer loaded it and **the save continued**. Exit gate: CTest 43/43, 269 unit tests, ASan + UBSan clean. **Milestone 3b-2 complete** — tag `milestone-3b2`, fast-forward merged into `main` (user choice), branch deleted, CTest 43/43 on `main`.
- **M3c brainstorming (2026-09-25)** — user decisions: finish line for all of 3c = every mGBA suite group at 100% except Timing ≥ mGBA's 1708/2020; split into **3c-1 right values** and **3c-2 right timing**; keypad IRQ, link port 'no cable' and Stop mode go into 3c-1; suite results checked by the test runner reading the score digits off the screen (`--suite <group>`, targets in `tests/suite_targets.json`); a check that proves timing-dependent moves to 3c-2 only with the user's OK. **mGBA suite downloaded with approval:** `https://s3.amazonaws.com/mgba/suite-latest.zip` (88,220 bytes, SHA-256 `b802bb1a08566456f1820d09f8c5362ede30c838297fd05acdf862f1b1e790a5`, MIT) → `tests/roms/mgba/suite.gba` (524,288 bytes, gitignored; not yet in `fetch_test_roms.py` — task 1 of the plan adds it). **Baseline scores** (release build, measured with a throwaway key-script probe in the scratchpad): Memory 1459/1552, I/O read 12/130, Timing 362/2020 (its first check 'Calibration' fails), Timer count-up 342/936, Timer IRQ 0/90, Shifter 140/140, Carry 93/93, Multiply long 52/72, BIOS math 325/615 (ArcTan lines red), DMA 1184/1244, SIO register R/W 7/90, SIO timing 0/4, Misc 3/12; group 13 'Video tests' is a manual sub-menu with no score. Menu: DOWN ×index then A opens a group; failing checks are drawn in red; score top right. Design: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-25-milestone-3c1-values-design.md`; parent spec milestone table updated.
- **M3c-1 planning (2026-09-25)** — with the user's approval, shallow clones of `mgba-emu/suite` (MIT) and `mgba-emu/mgba` (MPL-2.0) were put in the session scratchpad **for reading only** (nothing copied into the project); the multiply-carry research code (zaydlang/multiplication-algorithm, zlib) was read from GitHub. Findings now in the spec: the suite logs every failing check as text into SRAM (use `--save`); the suite's text is BG1 tiles numbered char−32, so the runner reads the score from the tile map (replaces the glyph-matching idea); causes of all 93 memory failures (HLE CpuSet/CpuFastSet align addresses — the BIOS doesn't; DMA must align in the save area; open bus); ArcTan/ArcTan2 rules verified against all 119 suite rows in Python; the multiply-carry port verified on 3M random inputs + all 72 suite checks; the link-port register model verified against all 90 suite rows; multiplayer transfers never finish with no cable (hardware); the 4 SIO timing checks include ~121 cycles of IRQ overhead, so they may need 3c-2 (ask the user if not 4/4 after Task 7). Every suite group finishes within 224 frames.

- **M3c-1 Task 1** — suite tooling: `tools/fetch_test_roms.py` fetches `suite-latest.zip` (size + SHA-256 pinned) and keeps `tests/roms/mgba/suite.gba` (both the 'already there' and the fresh-download paths checked; identical file); test runner `--keys <frame:KEY[+KEY],...>` (frames must increase; bad entry → usage, exit 3) and `--suite <group> [--suite-min n]` (DOWN presses + A, score read from the BG1 tile map, prints `SUITE <group> p/t`); `tests/suite_targets.json` (baseline minimums + totals); root `CMakeLists.txt` adds one `mgba_suite_<group>` CTest per group (0.4-3 s each in the debug build). CTest 56/56. (Tool note: long Python edit scripts inside a Bash heredoc sometimes fail with 'unexpected EOF'; writing the script to the scratchpad with Write and running it works.)

- **M3c-1 Task 2** — open bus from the prefetched opcode: `bus_open_bus()` in `core/src/mem/bus.c` (ARM: word at $+8; Thumb: $+4 repeated for EWRAM/palette/VRAM/ROM, BIOS/OAM and IWRAM by alignment per GBATEK) read with a side-effect-free `pipeline16` that only touches real memory and falls back to the last fetched opcode (`bus.open_bus`), so a PC in unmapped space cannot recurse; unmapped regions and BIOS past 0x3FFF use it. Old test `bus_unmapped_reads_return_open_bus` replaced by `tests/unit/test_open_bus.c` (6 tests). 274 unit tests; suite memory 1459 → **1479/1552** (exactly the 20 predicted BIOS/unmapped out-of-bounds checks); io/dma/timing/misc unchanged. CTest 56/56.

- **M3c-1 Task 3** — I/O read rules: `core/src/mem/io_rules.c` (range list → 512-entry table built by `io_init_tables()` in `gba_create`): readable registers with masks, unused slots that read 0, everything else open bus; `io_read` applies them on top of the stored value (`read16` stays the raw source, so byte-write merging keeps hidden bits); addresses from 0x04000400 up read open bus. Test helper `tu_raw16`; three old tests switched to raw reads / BG2CNT (they read write-only registers or BG0CNT's hidden bit 13). 281 unit tests. Suite: io 12 → **130/130**, sio-regs 7 → 25/90 (the zero slots); all 26 frame + 4 sound fingerprints unchanged. CTest 56/56. **Batch 1 (Tasks 1-3) done.**

- **M3c-1 Task 4** — memory group: HLE `CpuSet` keeps odd source/destination addresses (32-bit units through `bus_read32/bus_write32` like LDMIA/STMIA — the bus aligns, the save area keeps exact addresses; 16-bit units through `cpu_load_half`, so a misaligned LDRH rotates), `CpuFastSet` likewise; DMA `copy()` aligns its addresses to the unit size before each access (also in the save area). 4 new tests (3 failed first; `hle_cpu_set_words_from_an_odd_address_read_the_aligned_word` already passed — rounding down gives the same answer there — kept as a guard). 285 unit tests. Suite memory 1479 → **1552/1552**; dma unchanged (1184). CTest 56/56.

- **M3c-1 Task 5** — BIOS maths: `hle_arctan(tan, &r1, &r3)` takes the full 32-bit input and does the polynomial in wrapping 32-bit arithmetic (`mul32`, no signed-overflow UB), returns a sign-extended 16-bit result and hands back `a`/`b`; `hle_arctan2(x, y, &r1)` uses `(n << 14) / d` in 32 bits (`ratio`, INT32_MIN/-1 guarded) and 64-bit comparisons; SWI 0x09 sets r1 = a, r3 = b; SWI 0x0A sets r1 = inner a (y unchanged when x or y is 0), r3 = 0x170. Tests use rows of the suite's table. 287 unit tests. Suite bios-math 325 → **615/615**; `frame_tonc_swi_demo` unchanged (no re-approval needed). CTest 56/56.

- **M3c-1 Task 6** — multiply carry flag: `core/src/cpu/mul_carry.{h,c}` (carry-only port of zaydlang's multiplication-algorithm, zlib licence, credited in the file; the exact file checked during planning against the reference on 3M random inputs); ARM MULS/MLAS/UMULLS/UMLALS/SMULLS/SMLALS and Thumb MUL set C from it (operands read before Rd is written; V untouched). Old test renamed `arm_multiply_flags_keep_overflow_and_set_the_booth_carry` (C = 0 for -1 x 6). 291 unit tests. Suite multiply-long 52 → **72/72**; carry 93/93 and shifter 140/140 still full; jsmolka arm/thumb pass. CTest 56/56. **Batch 2 (Tasks 4-6) done.**

- **M3c-1 Task 7** — link port with no cable: `core/src/sys/sio.{h,c}` (mode from RCNT/SIOCNT; per-mode read rules; SIODATA32 written only in normal-32; normal mode with the internal clock schedules `EV_SIO` after bits x 8 or 64 cycles, then receives all 1s, clears the start bit and raises the serial IRQ if enabled; external clock and multiplayer never finish; clearing start cancels), `sio_t` in `struct gba`, `EV_SIO` in `sched.h`, `io.c` routes 0x120-0x12E and 0x134-0x15E. **Plan test bug fixed:** two tests read the start time before the SIOCNT write, which itself costs 1 cycle — they now read it after (plan updated). `sio_external_clock_and_multiplayer_never_finish` and `sio_clearing_start_cancels_a_transfer` passed before the code (nothing happened then either) — kept as guards. 296 unit tests. Suite sio-regs 25 → **90/90**. **sio-timing stays 0/4: every check is exactly 24 cycles short** (Normal8/256k 0x261 vs 0x279, Normal8/2M 0xA1 vs 0xB9, Normal32/256k 0x861 vs 0x879, Normal32/2M 0x161 vs 0x179) — the transfer times are exact, the constant gap is interrupt/BIOS overhead (3c-2 timing). Shown to the user: **user decision (2026-09-25): the SIO timing group moves to 3c-2** (spec, parent spec and plan updated). CTest 56/56.

- **M3c-1 Task 8** — keypad interrupt: `core/src/sys/keypad.{h,c}` `keypad_check()` (KEYCNT bit 14 enables, bit 15 AND/OR over bits 0-9; raises IRQ_KEYPAD), called from `gba_set_keys` and on KEYCNT writes (`REG_KEYCNT` case in `io.c`). 4 tests (3 failed first; `keypad_irq_needs_its_enable_bit` passed before — nothing happened then either). 300 unit tests, CTest 56/56.

- **M3c-1 Task 9** — Stop mode: `irq_t.stopped`, `irq_stop()` / `irq_stop_wake()` (keypad, serial or cartridge IRQ flagged and enabled in IE); HALTCNT bit 7 (byte write, or bit 15 of a halfword write to 0x300) and HLE SWI 0x03 enter Stop (Halt unchanged); `gba_run_frame` returns at once while stopped (CPU loop also ends mid-frame when Stop begins) and `apu_stopped_frame()` hands out one frame of silence (548 then 549 samples: the 320-cycle remainder carries over), so audio-paced frontends keep 60 fps. 4 tests, all failed to build first. 304 unit tests, CTest 56/56. **Batch 3 (Tasks 7-9) done.**

- **M3c-1 Task 10** — exit gate: CTest 56/56 with ASan + UBSan (no reports), 304 unit tests. Final suite scores: memory 1552/1552, io 130/130, shifter 140/140, carry 93/93, multiply-long 72/72, bios-math 615/615, sio-regs 90/90 (all 3c-1 groups full); 3c-2 groups at their baselines: timing 362/2020, timer-countup 342/936, timer-irq 0/90, dma 1184/1244, misc 3/12, sio-timing 0/4 (moved to 3c-2). Release build speed: ~970 fps headless on Tonc m7_demo and on the suite (~16x real time). **User checks (2026-09-25):** opened the suite in `gbaview` and saw full scores in all 3c-1 groups; with their OK, played their game A hack (menu without the dead-battery message, controls/sound/speed normal), saved in-game, closed, reopened — the viewer loaded the .sav and the save carried over. **Milestone 3c-1 complete** — tag `milestone-3c1`, fast-forward merged into `main` (user choice), branch deleted, CTest 56/56 on `main`.

- **M3c-2 brainstorming (2026-09-25)** — branch `milestone-3c2` created. Failure logs of the 3c-2 groups (`--suite <g> --save`) and the suite/mGBA sources in the scratchpad showed: Timing's Calibration fails on every prefetch ('P') row and on the N/S settings (we charge S instead of N after loads/stores; no pipeline-refill S after branches); Timer IRQ reads 0x44 everywhere (IRQ taken too early, and the handler path 13 cycles too fast — same cause as the SIO timing 24-cycle gap); 48 of 60 DMA failures = DMA from ROM must always increment the source, 12 = DMA from the BIOS area expecting 0xDEADDEAD; Misc = DMA-time open bus + H-blank flag 3 cycles off. **The misc group's log prints 'Got <hardware> vs <ours>'** (the suite swaps doResult's arguments there). mGBA facts: GBA_IRQ_DELAY 7; loads/stores add N−S to the next fetch; `GBAMemoryStall` prefetch estimate; timer reads offset by 2. User decisions: per-instruction timing like mGBA (not per bus cycle); changed fingerprints — Claude re-checks and re-approves identical-looking ones, shows the user real changes; speed guard ≥ 10× real time (release build); finish line as agreed (Timer count-up, Timer IRQ, DMA, Misc, SIO timing 100%, Timing ≥ 1708/2020). Design written.

- **M3c-2 planning (2026-09-25)** — the whole design was **prototyped first** in a throwaway git worktree (`<scratchpad>/proto`, branch `proto-3c2`; reproducible with `<scratchpad>/proto_steps.py`; delete both after the milestone: `git worktree remove --force <path>; git branch -D proto-3c2`, or `git worktree prune` if the scratchpad is gone). Cumulative scores per planned task are in spec §8 (final prototype: timing 1796, count-up 513, timer-irq 71, dma 1244, misc 6, sio-timing 0; 3c-1 groups still full; 321 unit tests green; only the audio of own_snd_psg and tonc_snd1_demo changes; ~790 fps release). Findings: jumps need N+S refill with a sequential next fetch; multiplies also make the next fetch N; LDM crossing into ROM is N; timers start 2 cycles after the write; BIOS routines = mGBA formula − 8; DMA takes the bus 3 cycles after the write while the CPU runs on (bus takeover); ROM DMA sources always increment (fixed all 60 DMA checks); mGBA's prefetch estimate took timing 1376 → 1796; IRQ delay 5 (sweep 2–8; 8 gives SIO timing 4/4 but costs count-up); a halted CPU must wake only when the request arrives (waking early hung count-up in IntrWait); H-blank flag/DMA at 1008, IRQ −6. mGBA's own leaderboard scores: count-up 610–744, timer-irq 70, dma 1232, timing 1520–1708 — **the earlier '100% on every group' finish line was unrealistic (Claude's mistake in the 3c brainstorm); the user chose mGBA level + a time-boxed tuning task (Task 8)**.

- **M3c-2 Task 1** — CPU cycle counts: `bus.data_access` flag (set by every `bus_read*/bus_write*` and by multiplies; code fetches no longer go through `bus_read*`), `bus_code_cycles()`; `cpu_step` charges the pipeline refill (N at the target + S after it, next fetch sequential) after branches and IRQ entry, and makes the next fetch non-sequential after a data access or multiply; LDM/STM/PUSH/POP accesses that start a new 16 MiB region are non-sequential. New `tests/unit/test_cycles.c` (7 tests; the straight-line one passed before the code, the other 6 failed as planned). 311 unit tests. Suite: timing 362 → **1048** (exactly the prototype), timer-countup 342 → **362** (prototype 367 — within the plan's 'a few points'); other groups unchanged, 3c-1 groups still full; targets raised. CTest 54/56: only `frame_own_snd_psg` (audio now `c36097d6c07318f5`) and `frame_tonc_snd1_demo` (audio now `47d45f9d2fe6c495`) fail, pictures unchanged — expected, re-approved in Task 9; until then use `bash tools/dev.sh test -E "snd_psg|snd1_demo"`.
- **M3c-2 Task 2** — timer start delay: `timer_write_control` sets `start = now + 2` when a timer starts (the overflow event moves with it); `timer_read_counter` returns the frozen count until `start`. New test `timer_starts_counting_two_cycles_after_the_write` (failed first: read 2 at t+2). 312 unit tests. Suite: timing 1048 → **1060**, timer-irq 0 → **44** (both exactly the prototype), timer-countup 362 → **421** (prototype 410 — better); 3c-1 groups still full; targets raised. CTest 54/54 (sound pair excluded).
- **M3c-2 Task 3** — BIOS routine times: `hle_div_cycles/hle_sqrt_cycles/hle_arctan_cycles/hle_arctan2_cycles` in `core/src/bios/hle_math.c` (mGBA's measured formulas, with the multiplier early-termination `mul_wait`); `hle_swi` charges formula − 8 for Div/DivArm/Sqrt/ArcTan/ArcTan2, 8 for CpuSet/CpuFastSet (plus 1 internal cycle per unit in their loops), 20 for the rest. 2 new tests (build error first, as planned). 314 unit tests. Suite: timing 1060 → **1200** (exactly the prototype); others unchanged, 3c-1 groups still full; `frame_tonc_swi_demo` unchanged. CTest 54/54 (sound pair excluded). **Batch 1 (Tasks 1-3) done** — shown to the user, who said continue.
- **M3c-2 Task 4** — DMA timing: an immediate DMA sets `dma.due = now + 3` (EV_DMA at that time); the CPU runs on, and every CPU bus access/fetch first runs a due transfer (`dma_bus_due` in `dma.h`, checked at the top of all `bus_read*/bus_write*/bus_fetch*`; `dma.active` stops the DMA's own accesses from re-entering); the old flat 2-cycle start delay is gone; a transfer ends with 2 idle cycles unless source and destination are both cartridge ROM; a cartridge-ROM source (0x08-0x0D) always increments. Old tests updated for the 3-cycle start (`run_pending` in `test_dma.c`, `dma3` in `test_eeprom.c`); `dma_immediate_runs_before_the_cpu_moves_on` still passes unchanged. 2 new tests (both failed first). 316 unit tests. Suite: timing 1200 → **1376**, dma 1184 → **1244/1244** (both exactly the prototype); others unchanged, 3c-1 groups still full. CTest 54/54 (sound pair excluded; their pictures still pass, only the same two audio hashes differ).
- **M3c-2 Task 5** — prefetch buffer (mGBA's `GBAMemoryStall` estimate): `bus_t` gains `prefetch_hint`, `off_rom`, `prefetch_end`; data accesses below 0x08000000 and `bus_idle` count off-cartridge cycles (code fetches do not); multiplies set the hint; at the end of an instruction that made a data access with the hint set, `bus_prefetch_stall()` (only with WAITCNT bit 14 and PC in 0x08-0x0D) credits the cycles the buffer saves and makes the next fetch sequential; a branch empties the buffer (`prefetch_end = 0`). As in the prototype, a cartridge data access or a WAITCNT write does not separately empty it (spec §3 mentions these; the estimate did not need them). New test `prefetch_buffer_hides_cartridge_waits_after_a_ram_load` (failed first: on = off). 317 unit tests. Suite: timing 1376 → **1796/2020** (exactly the prototype; finish line 1708 reached); others unchanged, 3c-1 groups still full. CTest 54/54 (sound pair excluded; pictures still pass, same two audio hashes).
- **M3c-2 Task 6** — interrupt delay: `irq_t` gains `pending` (IE & IF), `ime_on`, `visible_at`; a request is new when IE & IF becomes non-zero or IME switches on while one is pending, and the CPU sees it `IRQ_DELAY` = 5 cycles later (`irq_line`/`irq_wake` check `now >= visible_at`); `irq_changed()` is called on IE/IF/IME writes (`io.c` write16 + the IF byte write) and in HLE RegisterRamReset; `irq_raise_at()` lets timer overflow and H-blank/V-blank/V-count events count from their own time; a halted CPU jumps to `visible_at` and wakes then if that is before the next event. `cpu_takes_enabled_irq_before_next_instruction` (test_cpu.c) now calls `irq_changed` and waits 5 cycles. 3 new tests (build error first, as planned). 320 unit tests. Suite: timer-countup 421 → **513**, timer-irq 44 → **71** (both exactly the prototype; Timer IRQ finish line ≥ 70 reached), no TIMEOUT; timing 1796, dma 1244, misc 3, sio-timing 0 unchanged; 3c-1 groups still full. CTest 54/54 (sound pair excluded; pictures still pass, same two audio hashes). **Batch 2 (Tasks 4-6) done** — shown to the user, who said continue.
- **M3c-2 Task 7** — H-blank at cycle 1008: `PPU_HDRAW_CYCLES` 960 → 1008 (DISPSTAT H-blank flag, the H-blank event and H-blank DMA), H-blank IRQ requested 6 cycles before the flag (`irq_raise_at(..., when - 6)`); drawing still happens once per line. `ppu_hblank_flag_and_irq` (test_ppu.c) now checks 1007/1008. New test `hblank_flag_at_1008_and_its_irq_six_cycles_earlier` (both failed first). 321 unit tests. Suite: misc 3 → **6/12** (exactly the prototype); others unchanged, 3c-1 groups still full. CTest 54/54 — all 26 frame fingerprints unchanged (incl. Tonc `irq_demo`); sound pair still only differs in audio.
- **M3c-2 Task 8** — time-boxed tuning (details and the still-failing list in spec §9). Kept: (1) a started timer's start is rounded down to its prescaler period — the prescaler is one shared divider (`timer_write_control`); (3) a halted CPU wakes `HALT_WAKE_DELAY` = 3 cycles after a running CPU would see the request (`irq_wake_at()` in `irq.c`, used by `gba_run_frame`); (4') the H-blank IRQ is requested 7 cycles before the flag (was 6) — needed with (3) to keep Misc at 6. Dropped: stop-write freeze ±1 (lowered count-up and Timer IRQ), keeping the tick phase on settings-only rewrites (no effect), an H-blank flag computed at the exact read cycle (lowered Misc). Tests: new `prescaled_timer_starts_on_the_prescaler_grid`, `halted_cpu_wakes_three_cycles_after_a_running_one_would_see_the_request`; changed `irq_reaches_the_cpu_five_cycles_after_the_request` (no longer checks the halt wake), `halted_cpu_wakes_when_the_request_arrives` (wake at +8), `hblank_flag_at_1008_and_its_irq_seven_cycles_earlier`. 323 unit tests. **Final suite:** timing 1796, dma 1244/1244, timer-irq 71, **timer-countup 513 → 690** (aim 610; mGBA 610–744), misc 6, **sio-timing 0 → 4/4**; 3c-1 groups full; targets raised. CTest 54/54 (sound pair excluded); all 26 frames unchanged; `tonc_snd1_demo` audio moved again to `42b4378bf3f92a55` (own_snd_psg still `c36097d6c07318f5`) — Task 9. Tuning helpers (scratchpad, throwaway): `score.sh` (all 13 groups, release build), `log.sh` (failure log), `setexp.py`, `dbg.py` (temporary H-blank trace prints — removed). Results shown to the user, who said continue.
- **M3c-2 Task 9** — changed sound fingerprints: old sound recorded from `main` built in a temporary worktree (it reproduced the approved hashes), new from the branch, compared with `wav_probe.py` per 0.25 s window. **`own_snd_psg`**: same pitch in every window, RMS within 0.25 %, zero time shift → re-approved by Claude (audio `01494c669155c835` → `c36097d6c07318f5`). **`tonc_snd1_demo`**: RMS up to 15 % off per window because the whole tune starts ~29 ms (≈2 frames) later (envelope cross-correlation; after shifting it matches) — the plan's rule sent it to the user: **user listened to old and new in `gbaview` (2026-09-26) — 'they sound the same'** → re-approved (audio `ab8eb8ad9d5ad615` → `42b4378bf3f92a55`). Frame hashes of both unchanged. **Plan fix:** Step 3's ROM path for snd_psg is `../own_roms/snd_psg.gba` (relative to `tests/roms`), not `own_roms/...`. CTest **56/56** (nothing excluded any more). **Speed guard (release, 1200 frames, best of 3):** Tonc `m7_demo` 984 → **852 fps** (14× real time; guard 10×), suite 934 → 897 fps. **Batch 3 (Tasks 7-9) done** — shown to the user, who said continue.

- **M4a planning (2026-09-26)** — the user reviewed the spec ('It's great, continue'). The Gradle cache turned out partial (it came from another project): **with the user's approval, Gradle downloaded 15 small files (~750 KB, e.g. `com.squareup:javapoet:1.10.0`, `material-ripple` 1.8.1, `kotlinx-serialization-core-jvm` 1.7.3) from Google's Maven / Maven Central once**; everything since builds with `--offline`. The whole of 4a was **prototyped** in a throwaway worktree (`<scratchpad>/p4`, branch `proto-4a`, 2 commits; delete at the end of 4a: `git worktree remove --force <path>; git branch -D proto-4a`, or `git worktree prune` if the scratchpad is gone): all 32 fingerprints reproduced on Android, pixel-exact picture read-back, speed/pause/window tests 7/7 three times, the app driven by adb (start screen → picker → game; rotation, keys, home/back). Found while prototyping (all in the spec §9 and the plan): native code must be `-O2` even in debug (49 fps otherwise); `AChoreographer_postFrameCallback64` needs weak linking at minSdk 26; Git Bash needs `MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1` for adb phone paths; the pacer lost the first period's 0.3 ns; black-on-dark start screen text. The plan (9 tasks) is **generated from the prototype's files** (`<scratchpad>/gen_plan4a.py`) and was then **replayed task by task in a fresh worktree** with builds/tests after each task (PC 336, Kotlin 4, on-device 1 → 2 → 4 → 7 → 8, CTest 56/56, 0 C warnings); the replay found and fixed 3 plan bugs: `local.properties` must exist before the Gradle wrapper step; the adb tap helpers needed to wait/retry (the file picker's menu animates); a clean-start line for the picker. **Incident (2026-09-26): the user's real phone (phone A, Android 16) was plugged in over USB during the replay; Gradle's on-device tests ran on every connected device, so the test APKs were installed on the phone, the tests ran there (7/7 passed) and Gradle uninstalled them afterwards — nothing left on the phone (checked with `pm list packages`). Told the user; fix (user chose 'fix and keep going'): `dev.sh` exports `ANDROID_SERIAL=<emulator serial>` for every Android command, proven with the phone still plugged in (only the emulator ran the tests; a watcher saw nothing installed on the phone).** Rule: never run `./gradlew connected…`/`installDebug` or `adb` without targeting the emulator.
- **M4a Task 1** — plain-C platform pieces in `platform/android/`: `fit_rect` (largest centred 3:2 rectangle), `frame_pacer` (59.7275 Hz deadlines with the 5,013,504/2^24 ns remainder carried, restart when > 3 frames late, reset on pause), `triple_buffer` (lock-free 3 slots, `middle` word with a FRESH bit swapped by atomic exchange); PC tests `tests/unit/test_platform.c` (13, incl. a two-thread test: 20,000 frames never torn or older), built into `unit_tests` (plus `Threads::Threads`). Files written from the plan and checked byte-for-byte against the prototype. The 'fail first' step failed at CMake configure ('Cannot find source file') rather than at the missing headers the plan named — same cause. 336 unit tests, CTest 56/56.

On-device 4/4 (emulator only), 0 C warnings.
- **M4a Task 6** — the emulation thread: `emu_thread.{h,c}` (`emu_create` copies the ROM into a new core and sets the clock from the phone (`localtime_r` → UTC offset); the thread reads the atomic key mask, runs a frame, copies the picture into the triple buffer, drops the sound until 4b, counts frames, sleeps with `clock_nanosleep(TIMER_ABSTIME)` to the frame pacer's deadline; pause waits on a condition variable and resets the pacer; `emu_destroy` joins the thread), `jni_bridge` session functions (`loadRom`, `start`, `stop`, `pause`, `resume`, `setKeys`, `debugFrameCount`; one session, `loadRom` stops the previous one), androidTest `SessionTest` (speed over 5 s = elapsed × 59.7275 ± 3 frames; 20 rounds of load/start/pause (no frames while paused)/resume (frames again)/stop; empty ROM rejected — failed first: `Unresolved reference 'stop'/'loadRom'`). On-device 7/7 **three runs in a row** (emulator only), 0 C warnings. **Batch 2 (Tasks 4-6) done.**
- **M4a Task 7** — the render thread: `render_thread.{h,c}` (own pthread with an `ALooper`; messages through a pipe: 'W' window changed, 'Q' quit; `AChoreographer` frame callback (the 64-bit one on Android 10+, behind `__builtin_available`) takes the newest frame from the triple buffer, uploads it if fresh, draws at the surface's current size, swaps, re-posts; no new frame → the last one again; `render_set_window` hands over an `ANativeWindow` and returns only after the thread has switched (and let go of the old one), reporting whether OpenGL ES 3 could draw into it; the same window again (new size) keeps the surface; `EGL_CONTEXT_LOST` rebuilds everything), final `jni_bridge.c` (`start` creates the render thread; `setSurface` — a window before `start` is kept and handed over then; `stop` destroys the render thread before the emulator) and final `NativeBridge.kt`. New test `drawsIntoAWindowAndLetsGoOfIt` (jsmolka `stripes` into a 480×320 `ImageReader` surface: middle row not black; `setSurface(null)` returns) — failed first: `Unresolved reference 'setSurface'`. On-device 8/8 **three runs in a row** (emulator only), 0 C warnings; all native files now identical to the prototype's final versions.

- **M4b brainstorming (2026-09-26)** — branch `milestone-4b` created from `main`. User decisions: **AAudio** instead of the parent spec's OpenSL ES (deprecated) — shared, low-latency, no MMAP, minSdk stays 26; **approach A: the sound sets the pace** (emulation runs while the ring is under a 30 ms target, sleeps 1 ms at a time otherwise; ±0.5 % rate adjustment by fill; 4a's clock pacer only without sound). Design adds: cubic Hermite resampler with state across frames, lock-free SPSC `audio_ring` (8,192 frames), watchdog (ring not shrinking for 250 ms → reopen once → else clock pacing), reopen on device disconnect from the emulation thread, test hooks (`debugAudioStats`, `debugSetAudioEnabled`, `debugSimulateAudioDisconnect`). - **M4b planning (2026-09-26)** — the user reviewed the spec ('It's good, continue'). 4b was **prototyped** in a throwaway worktree (`<scratchpad>/p4b`, branch `proto-4b`; delete at the end of 4b): PC 13 new tests (349 total); on the phone emulator AAudio takes the legacy path — 48,000 Hz, bursts of 960 frames; on-device 15/15 in repeated runs. Found while prototyping: **the watchdog mistook the emulator's 350 ms cold start for a stuck stream → 2 s start-up grace**; AAudio's default buffer on the emulator was 4,360 frames (91 ms) → set to two bursts (1,920 frames, 40 ms), no dropouts; a stuck-stream test hook + test added so the watchdog is covered; test-file mistakes fixed (a −1 compare, a chunk over its buffer — caught by UBSan — the resampler's start-up allowance, `M_PI` not standard C). **The user listened to the prototype on the emulator (`snd_psg`, `snd1_demo`, their game A hack): 'Everything worked.'** (The emulator window was then closed by the user during a test run — not a crash; reruns 15/15.) The plan (5 tasks) is generated from the prototype (`<scratchpad>/gen_plan4b.py`; Task 3's in-between files derived by `<scratchpad>/make_v3.py`) and was **replayed task by task in a fresh worktree**: each task fails first as written, then PC 344 → 349, on-device 13 → 15 (twice each), 0 C warnings, CTest 56/56, files identical to the prototype. Spec updated (grace, buffer, hook, §8 prototype results).
- **M4b Task 1** (`c02ae52`) — `platform/android/rate_control.{h,c}` (output/input ratio from the buffer fill, ±0.5 % at most, fill clamped to [0, 1]) and `audio_ring.{h,c}` (lock-free single-producer/single-consumer ring, 8,192 stereo frames, full → the extra is dropped, `clear` only while the stream is stopped); PC tests `tests/unit/test_audio.c` (8: rate control 3, sound buffer 5 incl. a two-thread test moving 1,000,000 frames), built into `unit_tests`. Fail-first step failed at CMake configure ('Cannot find source file'), as the plan says. Files written from the plan. **344 unit tests, 0 failed; CTest 56/56; no warnings.** No surprises.
- **M4b Task 2** (`28731f5`) — `platform/android/resampler.{h,c}` (stereo int16, 4-point cubic Hermite / Catmull-Rom, keeps the last 4 input frames and the fractional position between calls, so pieces == one go; output rounded and clamped to int16; the window starts as silence, so the first ~6 output frames mix zeros in); PC tests `tests/unit/test_resampler.c` (5: 440 Hz stays 440 ± 0.5 Hz within 1 % loudness, constant in → exactly constant out, pieces == one go, ~48,000 frames out per second, no jump on a ratio change). Fail-first step failed at CMake configure, as planned. **349 unit tests, 0 failed; CTest 56/56; no warnings.** No surprises. **Batch 1 (Tasks 1-2) done** — shown to the user, who said continue.
- **M4b Task 3** (`bb61a7b`) — AAudio sound output: `platform/android/audio_out.{h,c}` (shared, low-latency, stereo int16 stream at the device's rate; data callback copies from the ring, fills the rest with silence, counts played/silent/loud frames; error callback only raises a 'disconnected' flag; AAudio's buffer set to two bursts); `emu_thread.{h,c}` with sound (each frame's samples → resampler at the rate-control ratio → ring; the thread waits 1 ms at a time while the ring holds > 30 ms; pause pauses the stream, empties the ring and resets the resampler; no stream → 4a's clock pacer); `jni_bridge.c` + `NativeBridge.kt` gain `debugAudioStats` / `debugSetAudioEnabled`; `aaudio` linked; androidTest `AudioTest` (5: stream opens, GBA speed paced by sound, sound really plays with no silence-filled frames after 0.5 s, pause/resume, clock pacing without sound). All 6 files checked byte-for-byte against the plan's code. **On-device 13/13 three runs in a row (emulator only), 0 C warnings;** `logcat`: `AAudio stream: 48000 Hz, burst 960 frames, buffer 1920 frames`, no crash. No surprises.
- **M4b Task 4** (`925d515`) — device changes and the watchdog (final `emu_thread.{h,c}`, `jni_bridge.c`, `NativeBridge.kt`): after each frame, a 'disconnected' flag from AAudio's error callback (or the test hook) makes the emulation thread close and reopen the stream (ring emptied, resampler reset, `reopens` + 1); in `sound_pace`, a ring that has not shrunk for 250 ms while playing = stuck stream → reopen once, the second time give up on sound (clock pacing); no watchdog in the first 2 s after a start/resume. - **M4b listening check, first part (plan Task 6, 2026-09-26)** — full run: CTest 56/56, 349 unit, Kotlin 4, on-device 15/15. Claude's check: `snd_psg` opened through the picker; the app on the game screen (black picture is right: `snd_psg` draws nothing), `dumpsys media.audio_flinger` showed the app's track playing, `logcat` one `AAudio stream: 48000 Hz, burst 960 frames, buffer 1920 frames`, no warnings, no crash. **User: 'Sound works great on all three'** (`snd_psg`, `snd1_demo`, their game A hack). **But in game A only the arrows and Enter worked** (stuck on the new-game screen). Told the user the key map (X = A, Z = B, A/S = L/R, Enter = Start, Backspace = Select); Z seemed to work, X not. Two `getevent -lt` recordings (user pressed X/Z; then held X 2 s, held Left 2 s, pressed Enter) showed the root cause: **the phone emulator's window sends letter keys as down+up pairs 0.1 ms apart, even while held** (Enter: one 256 ms press; held Left: 500 ms then ~34 ms repeats); Android delivered them to the app (`dumpsys input` RecentQueue), but the emulation thread reads the key mask once per frame (16.7 ms), so the game almost never saw them (Z 'working' was luck — same 0.1 ms taps). The user chose to fix it in 4b → new plan Task 5 (listening check renumbered to Task 6).

- **M4c trial by the user (2026-09-26):** 'Everything works, except' Home: reopening the app showed the start screen (save fine, but not where they were). Evidence (`logcat -b events`, `dumpsys activity`): the launcher created a **new MainActivity instance** on top of the paused one (3 stacked in task 41), because the task had been started by `dev.sh launch` (`am start -n`, no MAIN/LAUNCHER) — Android's root-intent mismatch. Reproduced: started by `am start -n` then the icon intent → 2 copies; started by the icon then the icon → 1 copy. Fix (prototype commit `83fbb29`): `android:launchMode="singleTask"` on MainActivity + `dev.sh launch` uses `-a MAIN -c LAUNCHER`. After: worst case (am start -n → game A → Home → icon) → 1 copy, the game shown where it was; on-device 29/29. Spec §5 updated.
